Assumption of Mary

Day 227 / 138 left · date num 69 / 33 / 49 / 23 · liturgical layer over the full MLB slate
Mary taken body and soul into heaven — the biggest feast of August. Elevation is the theme: rising, call-ups, career peaks.
Narratives: elevation/taken up (call-ups, promotions, career highs); Mary names (Maria, Marte, Marius); queen/crown; Mount Zion — tradition places the Dormition on Mount Zion (Abbey of the Dormition stands there today): MOUNT names carry the day; Stella Maris → Mariners; Hail Mary → walk-offs; white = the day's liturgical color
